Like in my garage, I’ll have everything shut off—no radio, no TV, no machines running, nothing—and I still hear dispatch clear as day.\n\nThey’ll call my operator number, and then it’s like, “he’s dangerous,” or “security’s coming,” that kind of thing. And the garage is quiet, you know, I keep my tools out there and I used to like the quiet, used to be where I could think… but then it cuts in like a transmission. It doesn’t feel like it needs noise around to happen.\n\nDoctor: Are there any other unusual beliefs or experiences you’ve been having that aren’t related to the dispatch voices or the warehouse system?\n\nPatient: I don’t know… not really, I guess. I mean, most of what feels strange to me ties back into that whole dispatch thing. It’s hard to separate it, you know?\n\nSometimes I’ll start thinking there must be something else going on, but then I end up right back at the same place in my head. Like I’ll be looking at the tools in the garage and remember I still need to oil the jack handle—my dad always said if you don’t maintain things, they fail right when you need them—and then I realize I’m not answering what you asked.\n\nSo… no, I can’t think of any other unusual beliefs or experiences outside of that. Not that I’ve noticed.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any other experiences where you felt like something was happening that others don’t seem to notice or understand, even if it wasn’t related to the dispatch voices or the warehouse?\n\nPatient: I mean… the main thing I keep coming back to is the dispatch stuff. Even when I’m just sitting in my garage, everything shut off—no radio, no TV, no machines, nothing making noise—I’ll hear them clear as day.\n\nThey’ll call my operator number, like I’m still on shift, and then say I’m dangerous or that security’s coming for me. And I know other people don’t seem to hear it, because it’s just me out there, but it feels real when it happens.\n\nI don’t know if that’s what you mean by something others don’t notice… I start thinking about the garage, the tools, how I used to keep it organized, and then it’s like that voice cuts through all of it.\n\nDoctor: Do you have any other unusual beliefs or ideas that most people might not agree with or understand, even if they aren’t connected to what you’ve been experiencing with the dispatch system?\n\nPatient: I mean… I don’t know if it’s “other,” because it all kind of runs through the same place for me. But the routing system at the warehouse—people say it’s just a computer, just assignments and scanners and all that—but to me it’s not just that anymore.\n\nIt’s been marking my moves. Like it knows where I am, what I’m doing, when I slow down, when I take a different path. And then the scanners… it’s like they send messages, little pushes, to get me out. Because I’m one of the old hands, you know? Been doing that kind of work a long time, and sometimes places don’t want the old guys around anymore.\n\nI know most people would say, “No, that’s just the system routing loads,” but… it doesn’t feel random to me. It feels aimed.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any beliefs or ideas that other people you’ve talked to didn’t seem to understand or agree with, even when they weren’t about the warehouse or the dispatch system?\n\nPatient: I mean… when you say “weren’t about the warehouse,” that’s where I get stuck, because that’s the part people don’t seem to understand. They’ll say it’s just routing, just scanners, just the computer doing its job.\n\nBut to me it’s not just a computer anymore. It’s been marking where I go, watching my moves, sending little messages through the scanners to push me out. Because I’m one of the old hands, and places don’t always want guys like me hanging around.\n\nI know people don’t agree with me when I say that. They look at me like I’ve gone off track, like when I start telling a story and end up talking about the forklifts we used back before everything had screens on it. But this… this feels aimed at me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any ideas or beliefs that you know other people would find strange or hard to believe, even if they weren’t related to the warehouse or the dispatch system?\n\nPatient: I keep trying to think of something separate from that, but it all circles back there. I’m here because that routing system at the warehouse… it isn’t just a computer to me anymore.\n\nPeople can say that sounds strange, and maybe it does to them, but I know it’s been marking my moves. Like when I take a different route or slow down, the scanners send these little messages, these pushes, like they’re trying to get me out because I’m one of the old hands.\n\nAnd I know I start talking about the warehouse again—my wife used to say I’d make a short answer into a whole road trip—but that’s the thing people don’t believe. They think it’s just work equipment. I don’t.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever believed something to be true that other people would find completely unbelievable, even when it wasn’t related to the warehouse or the dispatch system?\n\nPatient: I know you keep saying “not related,” and I’m trying, but the thing that people really don’t believe is that system at the warehouse. That’s why I’m here.\n\nIt’s not just a computer to me anymore. I know it’s been marking my moves—where I go, when I slow down, when I change up my route—and the scanners send these little messages, like nudges, trying to push me out. Because I’m one of the old hands there, you know? Been around long enough that sometimes they want you gone without saying it straight.\n\nOther people act like that’s completely unbelievable. But it doesn’t feel unbelievable to me. It feels aimed.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any beliefs or experiences that you know most people would find completely unbelievable, even when they weren’t related to the warehouse or anything happening there?\n\nPatient: I know you keep saying “not related to the warehouse,” and I’m trying to answer that, I am. It’s just… that’s the thing. That’s the one people look at me funny over.\n\nI’m here because that routing system at the warehouse isn’t just a computer to me anymore. I know it’s been marking my moves, watching where I go, and the scanners send messages—little pushes, like they’re trying to move me out. And it’s because I’m one of the old hands there.\n\nPeople say that sounds unbelievable, but to me it feels plain as day.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever believed something was happening or being done to you that most people would find impossible or completely illogical, like being watched by machines in a way that can’t be explained by normal means?\n\nPatient: Yes… that’s what I mean. The machines at the warehouse, the routing system, the scanners—people act like they’re just equipment, like they only beep and track inventory. But I know it’s more than that.\n\nIt’s been marking my moves. When I take a different path or I’m a little slower, the scanner gives these messages, these little signals, like it’s pushing me, correcting me, trying to get me out. And I know how that sounds. My wife used to say I’d read too much into things, but after you’ve worked a place that long, you know when something changes.\n\nI’m one of the old hands there, and it feels like the system knows that. Like it’s being used to move me along without anyone having to come right out and say it.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any experiences or beliefs where you felt like you were part of something much bigger—like a government plan, a secret mission, or that you had special abilities or a unique role that most people wouldn’t understand?\n\nPatient: Not like a government thing, no… I don’t think it’s that. I’m not saying I’ve got some secret mission or powers or anything.\n\nIt’s more the warehouse. I know I keep coming back to that, but that’s where it is. That routing system isn’t just a computer to me anymore. It’s been marking my moves, and the scanners… they send these messages, little signals, like they’re trying to push me out. Because I’ve been there a long time, one of the old hands, and sometimes places don’t want to say it straight.\n\nSo not “special abilities,” exactly. More like I got put in a role there without asking for it. Like the system knows me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that things in the world around you—like TV broadcasts, public announcements, or other people’s actions—were directed at you or had a special meaning for you, even when they didn’t seem to involve you directly?\n\nPatient: TV and announcements… not so much, no. I mean, I don’t sit there thinking the news anchor is talking to me or anything like that.\n\nBut people’s actions, and the things around me at work… that’s where it happens. The routing system at the warehouse, the scanners, the way they beep or send me to certain places—it feels directed at me. Like it’s not just random instructions. It’s marking my moves, watching what I do, and sending these little messages to push me out because I’ve been there so long, one of the old hands.\n\nAnd I know you asked about the world in general, but that’s the place where it shows up. That’s where I can feel it.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever believed something was happening that you know most people would say is impossible, like machines being able to read your thoughts or control your actions?\n\nPatient: I don’t think it reads my thoughts exactly… not like in a movie or something. But it knows what I’m doing. That routing system at the warehouse, it’s not just a computer to me anymore.\n\nI’ll take a turn or slow down, and then the scanner sends something, a beep or a message, and it feels like it’s marking me. Like it’s been keeping track of my moves and trying to steer me, push me out. I’ve been there a long time, one of the old hands, and places get funny about that. They don’t always come right out and say they want you gone.\n\nSo… control my actions, maybe not completely. But it’s pushing. It’s sending messages through the scanners. That’s why I’m here, because I can’t just ignore it anymore.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like the machines are doing something that most people would say is impossible, like putting thoughts into your mind or taking them out?\n\nPatient: Putting thoughts in or taking them out… no, I don’t know if I’d say it like that. That sounds too… science fiction, you know?\n\nBut the machines are doing something they shouldn’t be able to do. The routing system at the warehouse, the scanners—I'm here because it isn’t just a computer to me anymore. It’s been marking my moves. I’ll get sent one way, then the scanner beeps, and it feels like a message, like it’s saying, we see you, we know where you are.\n\nAnd I know people say, “It’s just the system,” but I’ve worked there long enough. I’m one of the old hands. You start to know when a place is trying to push you out without saying it straight. So not thoughts in my head exactly, but messages through the machines, yes."
